# Excalidraw Data

## Text Elements
While 4 more loudspeakers in parallel will result in a total impedance of 8/8 = 1 Ohm, this might become an issue if the voltage is kept constant, as it can generate an unsafe current level.
In other words: ^kZukt3tI

The reduction of impedance, by Ohm's law
creates a noticeable increase of current, which can result in excessive heat inside the speaker, which might damage the device.. It can also affect the amplifier


The smarter way to handle this would be placing these 4 speakers in another layout,.
We should deliver enough power but still using a safe amount of current ^sHys3Zxn

By observing the phase curve (red), we can check that it presents
two dips in frequency, aligning with the peaks from the bass reflex
impedance response, thus indicating ^dCzqTYmb
